2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%， 顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域 。 數據公布後 ， 市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫 。 聯準會理事鮑曼表示 ， 決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果 ； 舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示 ， 不宜對升息過於保守 ， 並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示 ， 在能源價格波動下 ，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決， 歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶 ， 周初 ， 外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢 ， 雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯 ，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶 ； 週二台股重挫近千點 ， 外資大幅賣超台股1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡 ， 總成交量91.2 億 ， 再創歷史新高 ； 週五外資持續匯出 ， 台幣貶至周內低點32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/19/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。上週人民幣中間價連續走升，週三中間價升至6.7249 創
三年八個月新高，惟市場靜待美國公布通膨數據，交投謹慎，人民幣僅小幅走升；
週三晚間，美國公布 9 月 CPI 年增率 3.7%、核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，雙雙高於預期，
帶動美元指數升至年內高點 102.945、 人民幣貶至周內低點6.7005； 然多位聯準會官
員發言表示聯準會不急於升息，令市場對於聯準會十月升息預期略有降溫，美元指
數自高點回落，激勵人民幣由貶轉升；臨近周末，聯準會主席華許於IMF 會議發言
並未指引十月聯準會政策利率走向，再度淡化市場對於聯準會十月升息預期，國際
美元走勢偏弱，人民幣走升觸及年內高點 6.6825，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6850，週
線升值 91 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6870，週線升值 60 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。就國際市場來看，美國仍面臨高通膨、年底可能將
再次升息，利差因素支撐美元指數於高檔震盪，限縮人民幣升值空間；就中國國內
市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人
民幣表現；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，匯價維持在五日均線之下交
投 ， 均線呈現空頭排列 ， 加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.6900 整數關卡 ， 顯示USD/CNH
空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6500-6.7000

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 先上後下。週初，受油價攀升推升美國通膨與利率預期帶動，美
元指數溫和走升創 6 月底以來新高，推升 USD/JPY 突破 158.50；週間，美國公布 9
月 CPI 高於預期，帶動美元指數升至 2025 年 5 月以來高點，也使 USD/JPY 最高來
到 158.94，然逢高獲利了結賣壓出籠使匯價回落；臨近週末，USD/JPY 一度下探至
157.53，隨後逢低買盤進場收復部分跌幅，終場收在 157.99，週線下跌 13 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、受美伊戰事推升汽柴油飆漲
與服務成本擴散影響，美國 9 月 CPI 及 PPI 月增漲幅均超出市場預期，加上初請失
業金人數連五週低於 20 萬人顯示勞動市場穩定，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月再次升
息，美日實質利差維持高檔，持續為USD/JPY 提供下檔支撐。二、被視為鴿派的日
本經濟財政大臣城內實明確表態，貨幣政策應交由日本央行自行判斷，並宣告日本
已擺脫長期通縮、轉型為增長導向經濟體。此番言論降低政治干預日銀進一步升息
的疑慮，在貨幣政策正常化的腳步下，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間。三、就技
術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐
前 ， 仍以區間震盪看待。 綜合以上 ， 預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高 。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：157.00-159.00 
前週交易區間 
157.53 158.94

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
歐元兌美元匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初，持續受法國財政赤字與歐債利差擔憂拖累，
EUR/USD 震盪走弱；週間，因美國9 月 CPI 高於預期，市場重新評估聯準會年底前
升息腳步，導致 EUR/USD 跳水最低來到 1.1112；臨近週末，儘管美國 PPI 與零售
銷售數據偏熱，市場優先反映美債殖利率回落與風險資產重新定價，美元多頭獲利
了結帶動 EUR/USD 谷底反彈 ， 最高來到1.1269， 終場收在1.1239， 週線上漲42 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、法國央行月度展望報
告指出，法國第三季 GDP 預估僅微幅成長 0.1%，印證近期高能源的成本已實質拖
累歐元區核心成員國增長。同時，歐洲央行總裁拉加德明確拒絕出手干預法債，要
求法國透過自身財政政策化解赤字困境；在明年法國大選前的財政隱憂與基本面疲
弱下，預計將令歐元上檔持續承壓。二、儘管歐洲央行管委溫施與總裁拉加德保留
12 月升息預期，加上德國基建與軍費支出為歐元提供部分下檔保護，但市場穩固預
期聯準會年底前將再升息一次。在美國經濟韌性與高利差優勢主導下，預計將限縮
歐元反彈空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 自低點反彈並站回五日線
之上，然而中長期均線下彎仍維持空頭排列，RSI 指標亦處於 50 中軸下方，顯示整
體空方趨勢尚未扭轉，反彈至月線附近恐面臨較大賣壓。綜合以上，預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1300 
前週交易區間 
1.1112 1.1269

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115. 10.12-115.10.16 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.020~2.025% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ，本週央行連續減發定存單使市場資金寬鬆 ， 雖外資大舉匯
出，資金供給仍充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週五央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.58%， 低於市場
預期區間 1.60%-1.62%，較上期標售結果下跌 5.8 個基本點。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 5 年期指標券 A15108 週二搓合成交在 1.88%，後隨美債利率滑落，加上本週
2 年期公債標債結果提振市場持券信心，帶動 10 年期指標券 A15109 週四向
下成交在 2.02%~2.025%。 
在銀行去化短期資金及補充 LCR 之需求下 ， 週三2 年期公債得標利率為1.65%，
落在市場預估區間下緣 ， 投標倍數回升至2.86 倍 ， 銀行業得標比重上升至88%。
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率大幅回檔，促使台債交投偏空氛圍轉趨和
緩 ， 惟在央行維持緊縮貨幣政策下 ， 買盤積極追價的意願不強 ， 預期短線台債
殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

## Document 091: 2027年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート ～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート ～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/3 
2027 年春闘の初動モメンタム・アップデート 
～経労委報告原案と産別要求からも賃上げ意欲を確認～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 経団連による、2027 年春闘に向けての経労委報告原案が判明した。高い賃上げの「社会全体
への浸透」を掲げており、大企業を中心に高い賃上げが定着しつつあるなか、その動きを中
小企業などにも広げることに重点を置く姿勢がうかがえる。また、筒井経団連会長の発言か
らは、 ベア実施の検討をスタンダードとする従来の方針が維持されていることも確認できる。 
○ 流通やサービス、繊維などの労働組合で構成されるＵＡゼンセンも定昇込み６％、ベア４％
など、前年並みの高い賃上げ要求を掲げた。労使とも、賃上げに向けての姿勢にトーンダウ
ンはみられない。 
○ 一方、賃上げ率のさらなる加速を示唆する材料は現時点で多くない。連合の要求目安は前年
より強まったものの、経団連は賃上げ率の加速までは示していないとみられ、ＵＡゼンセン
の要求率は前年と同水準である。2027 年春闘では、 ５％近傍という高い賃上げ率を維持でき
るかが焦点となる。 
○ 日銀の植田総裁は、2027 年春闘で２％のインフレ率を前提とした賃金設定が行われることの
重要性を指摘した。2026 年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍化するなかでも高い賃上げが続くかど
うかは、２％の物価安定目標の定着や今後の金融政策を判断するうえで重要となる。

経労委報告原案は、高い賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」を掲げる 
10 月22 日のレポート（2027 年春闘、初動のモメンタムは現時点で良好 ～労働側、経営側とも
昨年からのトーンダウンはみられず～ | 新家 義貴 | 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所）では、連
合の基本構想や経団連の経労委報告骨子案などをもとに、2027 年春闘に向けた賃上げ姿勢は労使
双方とも前年からのトーンダウンはみられないと評価したが、その後、経団連の経労委報告原案
やＵＡゼンセンの要求方針素案が明らかになった。本稿では、これらの新たな材料を踏まえ、春
闘の初動のモメンタムをアップデートする。 
10 月30 日、経団連が2027 年春闘に向けて取りまとめる「経営労働政策特別委員会報告（経労
委報告） 」の原案内容が報道された。報道によれば、原案では賃上げの力強いモメンタムを継続

### Page 2

2/3 
し、その動きを社会全体に浸透させるために取り組みを強化する方針が示された。特に、中小企
業が継続的・計画的な賃上げを実施できるかが重要とされ、適正な価格転嫁に加え、大企業が持
つ人材や技術を活用して中小企業の収益力向上を支援する必要性が指摘された。また、ＡＩの普
及を踏まえ、働き手が生み出した成果をどのように評価し、賃金などの処遇へ反映するかについ
て、見直しを求めている。生産性向上によって生み出された付加価値を賃金にどう配分するか
は、今後の労使交渉においても重要な課題となるだろう。 
経団連はこれまで、2023 年を賃上げの「起点」 、2024 年を「加速」 、2025 年を「定着」 、2026 年
を「さらなる定着」の年と位置付けてきた。今回の原案では「社会全体への浸透」が掲げられて
おり、大企業を中心に高い賃上げが定着しつつあるなか、その動きを中小企業などにも広げるこ
とに重点を置く姿勢がうかがえる。2026 年春闘向けの経労委報告でも、中小企業における構造的
な賃上げや適正な価格転嫁の重要性が強調されていたが、今回の原案では、大企業による人材・
技術面での支援にも言及するなど、賃上げの裾野を広げるための取り組みをより具体的に示して
いる。 
賃上げに対する経営側の基本姿勢も維持されている。2026 年春闘向けの経労委報告では、ベア
実施の検討を賃金交渉のスタンダードと位置付けていたが、筒井経団連会長は今年９月７日の記
者会見でも、 「ベースアップ実施の検討を引き続きスタンダードと位置付けていきたい」と述べて
いる。ベアに対する従来の方針が維持されていることは重要だ。今春以降のイラン情勢悪化を受
け、原材料費や物流費の上昇によって企業の賃上げ余力が圧迫されることが懸念されていたが、
少なくとも現時点で経団連が賃上げに慎重な姿勢へ転じた様子はうかがえない。

UAゼンセンも前年並みの高い要求を維持 
労働側でも、主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針が明らかになり始めた。10 月 27 日、流通やサ
ービス、繊維などの労働組合で構成されるＵＡゼンセンは、2027 年春闘の要求方針素案を公表し
た。正社員組合員については、定昇込みで６％、ベア等の賃上げ分で４％を基本とし、賃金水準
が最低基準に満たない組合などでは格差是正分を加えて７％の賃上げを要求する。また、短時間
組合員については、制度昇給分に加えて５％、制度昇給分が明確でない場合には７％を目安とす
るとのことである。 
これらの要求水準は、いずれも2026 年春闘から変わっていない。６～７％という要求水準は依
然として高いが、要求率を前年からさらに引き上げたわけではないことには注意が必要だ。連合
は 2027 年春闘に向けた基本構想で「６％中心、５％以上」と前年より強い要求目安を示したが、
実際には前年の段階で、すでに６％程度の賃上げを要求していた産別組織もある。今回の連合の
方針には、こうした既存の要求水準を追認する側面もあり、ＵＡゼンセンの要求方針はその一例
といえる。

前年からのトーンダウンはみられていないが、加速しているわけでもない  
以上を踏まえると、2027 年春闘の初動のモメンタムは引き続き良好と評価できる。経団連はベ

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ア実施の検討をスタンダードとする従来の方針を維持し、高い賃上げを中小企業などにも広げる
ことで「社会全体への浸透」を目指す姿勢を示している。労働側でも、連合が前年より強い要求
目安を掲げており、ＵＡゼンセンも前年並みの高い要求水準を維持した。イラン情勢悪化に伴う
コスト増加が懸念されるなかでも、労使双方の賃上げ姿勢に前年からのトーンダウンはみられな
い。 
もっとも、賃上げ姿勢が慎重化していないことと、賃上げ率がさらに高まることは別問題であ
る。経団連が重視しているのは高い賃上げの継続とその裾野の拡大であり、賃上げ率の加速を示
唆しているわけではないだろう。また、ＵＡゼンセンの賃上げ要求は高いが、要求率自体は前年
から据え置かれている。連合の要求目安は強まったものの、現時点で賃上げ率の加速を見込むだ
けの材料は揃っていない。エコノミストの予想を集計するＥＳＰフォーキャスト調査（10 月調
査）でも、2027 年春闘の賃上げ率予想は4.96％とされており、前年実績の5.18％から小幅に鈍化
するとの見方となっている（数値は厚生労働省ベース）。 
当面の焦点は、５％近傍という高い賃上げ率を維持できるかどうかにある。もっとも、今後の
経済・物価情勢によっては企業の賃上げ余力や労働側の要求姿勢も変化し得るため、先行きを慎
重に見極める必要がある。当面、自動車総連や電機連合など主要産業別労働組合組織の要求方針
が前年からどの程度変化するかが注目される。 
金融政策の観点からも2027 年春闘の動向は重要である。日銀の植田総裁は10 月 30 日の記者会
見で、基調的な物価上昇率が２％の物価安定の目標と概ね整合的な水準に達したとの認識を示し
た上で、2027 年春闘について、労使交渉でも２％のインフレ率が前提として織り込まれることが
必要との考えを示した。一方、2026 年の物価上昇率が過去２～３年より低くなる可能性を指摘
し、それが春闘の結果にどう影響するかを注視する姿勢も示している。 
ここ数年の春闘では、物価上昇に負けない賃上げを実現することが、高い賃上げ要求を支える
重要な要因となってきた。2026 年の物価上昇率は 2025 年と比べれば鈍化する見込みであり、こう
した観点からの賃上げ圧力はやや弱まる可能性がある。 
もっとも、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを前提として賃金を設定するよう
になれば、足元の物価上昇率が低下しても安定的な賃上げは持続しやすくなる。日銀は基調的な
物価上昇率が２％に「到達」したと判断したが、２％の物価安定目標が「定着」したとまでは判
断していない。その意味でも、2027 年春闘は、２％の物価上昇を前提とした賃金設定がどの程度
定着しているかを確認する機会となる。春闘に向けた労使双方の姿勢や実際の賃上げ動向は、今
後の日銀の金融政策判断においても重要な材料となるだろう。

一、 AI 需求帶動，第四季營收與獲利率續增  
3Q26 營收 495.32 億元， QoQ 增長 11.4% 、YoY 增長 49.7% ，連續
第七季季增，創下單季營收歷史新高。價格調整逐步反映，加上
AI 產品組合與營運效率改善，毛利率達 41.0% ，較前季提高 2.5
個百分點；營業利益率 29.5% ，EPS 為 5.83 元。投入成本提高抵
銷部分效益，但營收與毛利成長仍帶動獲利增加。

AI 相關應用占營收已由 1Q26 的 15%、2Q26 的 16%， 提高至3Q26
的 19.8% 。按公布占比計算，第三季 AI 相關營收約 98.07 億元，
較第二季約 71.13 億元增長 37.9% 。成長涵蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容，以
及電源端使用的薄膜與鋁電容，反映運算、通訊與供電需求共同
增加。工業營收亦已連續七季成長，車用維持正向，消費性營收
仍有成長，但速度低於其他應用。

第四季通常為消費性產品淡季，公司考量接單及在手訂單仍強，
指引營收、毛利率與營業利益率均較第三季小幅提高。本研究採
稼動率約 95% ，以既有調價遞延反映支撐淡季營收，預估 4Q26
營收 520.09 億元， QoQ 增長 5.0% 、YoY 增長 44.6% ，毛利率
42.78% 、營業利益率 31.43% 、EPS 為 6.36 元。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一、完整被動元件產品線， AI 相關由板端延伸至電源  
國巨透過產品開發及 Pulse 、KEMET 、奇力新等併購布局，產品涵
蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容、鋁電容、薄膜電容、電阻、磁性元件與感測
器，服務工業、運算、通訊、車用及消費性市場。 AI 設備從機房
供電、機櫃電源轉換到運算板，都需要處理電壓穩定、濾波、儲
能及訊號干擾；不同位置的電壓、空間與可靠度要求不同，使完
整產品組合有利於參與客戶多個供電環節。

3 
公司第三季 AI 相關應用占比接近兩成，已分布於多項產品線。隨
運算功耗提高，成長範圍由靠近 GPU 與 ASIC 的板端電容，擴大
至通訊設備、電源模組及機櫃供電。磁性元件可參與電源轉換及
濾波，電阻用於訊號與電流相關功能，感測產品則提供溫度等監
測需求。

供應能力也逐漸成為競爭條件。國巨的多產品、全球製造據點及
既有客戶關係，有助協調產品開發與後續供貨； TDK 與太陽誘電
討論先進 MLCC 、電感的共同開發及製造、採購合作，亦反映高
容值、高可靠度與穩定供應的重要性提高。

二、MLCC 與鉭電容為主軸，高容值與穩定性優勢擴大  
AI 運算功耗與供電密度增加，要求電容在有限空間內提供足夠電
容量，並維持供電穩定。 MLCC 具備小型化與高密度配置的優勢，
對高容值、小尺寸與可靠度的要求隨平台升級提高；鉭電容則具
備高容值與電容量穩定性優勢，可配合運算及通訊設備的不同低
壓電源設計，支應儲能與濾波需求。兩者實際搭配取決於電路位
置、電壓、體積與成本。

國巨透過 KEMET 具備鉭電容與多類電容產品布局，可配合客戶
選擇不同供電方案。當高容值 MLCC 供應吃緊或設計成本提高
時，其他電容可提供設計選項，但轉換仍須完成電路調整與驗證，
不能將短缺直接換算成鉭電容新增訂單。本研究以目前已觀察到
的產品成長、擴產與價格假設推估， 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容營收
分別為 628.36 億元及 669.79 億元， YoY 增長 65.6% 及 51.1% ，合
計營收占比由 2026 年的 44.67% 升至 51.96% 。 
圖一、各類別營收佔比

電源端亦提供後續產品升級機會。隨資料中心朝更高功率及 800V
直流供電方向發展，不同轉換位置將提高耐壓、溫度穩定性及效
率要求。高壓端與轉換後低壓端所需元件不同，國巨可透過陶瓷、
薄膜、鋁電容與磁性元件參與多段供電。

三、日韓接單與交期延長，高階電容供需偏緊延續  
日韓大廠的出貨與接單，顯示高階電容需求已反映在營運數據。
依， JEITA 公布最新 2026 年 7 月日廠被動元件全球出貨額 4,280
億日圓，年增 23.2% ；村田因 AI 伺服器用需求急速成長，村田製
作所將擴建 MLCC 工廠，滿足需求增加。村田之前 4 月宣布，計
畫在截至 2028 年 3 月為止的 2 年 期 間，對MLCC 生產設備投資約
800 億日圓，提升 10-15% 產能。關於投資計畫，這次中島規巨表
示，「正評估能將投資時程提前多少」 。表示「原先預估位於島根
縣出雲市的工廠產能要到 2030 年才會滿載，如今卻可能提前至
2026 年就達到滿載」

根據 Future Electronics 最新資料亦顯示交期延長 。 村田低於1μF
產品交期由 9 月 26–28 週延長至 10 月 30–36 週，高於 1μF 及
車規產品由 26–30 週延長至 30–36 週； TDK 低於 1μF 產品由
26–36 週延長至 30–40 週，高 於1μF 及車規產品由 26–48 週調
整至 30–48 週。三星電機上述三類產品均由 30 週以上調整至 52
週，價格維持向上。國巨低於 1μF、高於 1μF 及車規產品交期
均維持 30–52 週，交期與價格趨勢持續向上，整體而言， AI 伺服
器與電源規格升級持續帶動被動元件需求，交期不僅維持高檔，
主要 MLCC 廠及部分電阻產品更進一步拉長，漲價品項亦有增
加，延續供不應求的正向看法。

5 
高效能晶片的 AI 伺服器中不可或缺的元件。隨著大型科技公司準
備明年推出下一代 AI 伺服器，三星電機預計 AI 用 MLCC 的需求
將達到今年的約八倍 ， 這項預測較上一季公佈的超七倍有所上調。

國巨本次法說表示，全球代理商庫存在過去三個月由約五個月降
至四個月，大中華區代理商維持兩個月以下。供應商接單偏強、
交期延長與通路庫存下降相互呼應，增加客戶提前確保供應的需
求。

四、滿載提高既有產出， 2027 年擴產分批支應訂單

本研究預期 2027 年高階電容供需仍偏緊，主要考量需求成長、產
品升級與新增供給之間存在時間差。 AI 平台提升功耗與供電密
度，對電容量及小型化提出更高要求；高階產品也會改變產能配
置，設備投入增加未必等比例轉化為可交付的標準品顆數。三星
規劃菲律賓新廠於 2027 年量產高附加價值 MLCC，同時仍預期高
階供應偏緊，村田將兩年擴產 10-15% 計畫考慮提前，皆是供不應
求現象。

國巨新增產能自第二季及下半年陸續開出，第一季主要仍依靠既
有產能與去瓶頸。本研究採 MLCC 、鉭電容年底有效產能較目前
增加 15% ，全年平均增幅為 7.5% 。因此，年底產能增加 15% 不等
於全年供給增加 15%； 加上第三季訂單出貨比仍接近 2， 新增供給
需先支應既有接單與後續需求持續。

基於交期延長、高階產品需求增加及擴產分批到位，本研究假設
國巨於 2027 年針對 MLCC 及鉭電容有機會再調價 ， 根據今年國巨
2026 年 2 月調高晶片電阻價格 10% 至 20% ，並同步調整鉭電容；
7 月調價範圍擴至各產品線，假設幅度約 15% 至 25% ，先假設明
年特定產品再調升 15% ，並假設於下半年完整反應。此輪假設調
價僅涵蓋兩項電容，屬同規格售價提高，與既有升規組合及 2026
年調價全年化分開計算 。(註：15% 幅度及生效時點為本研究假設，
並非公司已公布指引。 )

成本方面，公司表示鉭粉價格已趨穩定，且已進行策略採購，支
持供貨及成本管理。

二、預估 2027 年營收 2,498.39 億元， EPS 為 35.71 元 
2027 年各季維持 100% 稼動率 ，MLCC 與鉭電容有效產能自第二季
起逐步增加 ， 並沿用高階產品組合提升的假設 。 第一季營收562.55
億元， QoQ 增長 8.2% ，反映稼動率由 95% 升至 100% 、調價剩餘
效益及產品組合提升。另假設第二季僅 MLCC 及鉭電容再調價
15%，第三季起完整反映，第四季維持新價格，貢獻全年新增營收
96.39 億元。第二至第四季營收分別為 587.39 、659.50 、688.95 億
元，全 年 營 收2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長 35.7%。 預估毛利率47.09%、
營業費用 247 億元、費用率 9.89% ，營業利益 929.55 億元， YoY
增長 77.7% ；業外淨收入 18.60 億元、基本稅率 22% ，全年 EPS
為 35.71 元， YoY 增長 72.7% 。

EQUITY: FABLESS
MediaTek   2454.TW 2454 TT
Global Markets Research
30 October 2026
Dispels recent market noises with upside surprise; retain Buy
Quick Note
Maintain Buy and TP of TWD5,800
We recently noted that MediaTek’s earnings call could prove to be a positive catalyst for 
its share price, considering a number of unfounded concerns and noises had taken center 
stage before the call (Preview: market concerns overdone ). However, we believe some of 
MTK’s messages during earnings call turned out to be even more bullish than we had 
expected, including: (1) TPU v.8 contribution of USD2.5bn in 4Q26E (vs. previous 
guidance of more than USD2bn , and Street concerns around MTK potentially missing the 
USD2bn target); (2) management now expects ASIC sales in 2027E to reach the high end 
(USD16bn) of its prior guidance range (USD14-16bn) vs. our forecast of USD14bn; (3) 
TPU v.9 schedule is on track for 1Q28E mass production (a powerful clarification vs. 
Street expectation of 1-2 quarters’ delay); (4) management is very confident about v.9 
EMIB-T future yield trend after resolving 2-3 key issues (implying no CoWoS back-up plan, 
vs. Street expectation of a CoWoS-based v.9 project being opened); (5) Mediatek 
announced that it has secured a 2nd ASIC customer with sales contribution from 2028E; 
(6) ASIC-driven operating leverage will kick in from 4Q26E (guidance suggesting 19% 
OPM in 4Q26E, vs our estimate of 18.6% and 3Q26’s 16%; and management expects 
2027E OPM to be above 20%, vs our estimate of 22%). The only downside surprise might 
be its 4Q26E sales guidance, which suggests 25%-32% q-q growth vs. our estimate of 
34% q-q. We would watch if this is a low bar for MediaTek to beat. GPM guidance of 
45%+-1.5% is 1pp lower than previous quarter’s range (46%+-1.5%; and 46.3% actual in 
3Q26) due to ASIC — in line with our forecast of 45.4%. Overall, we expect the share 
price to react positively to the earnings call and, thus, reiterate our Buy rating and 
TWD5,800 TP, based on 25x unchanged 2027-28 EPS estimate. The stock is currently 
trading at 16.9x.

Results summary
3Q26 revenue was TWD168.1bn (+10.4% q-q, +18.3% y-y), above the high end of
the TWD152.2–159.8bn guidance.
The beat came mainly from better-than-expected customer demand and
improving supply.
○
Growth was driven by volume production of several new Smart Edge projects and
higher DRAM content in TV SoC.
○
•
GM was 46.3% (+0.1pp q-q, -0.2pp y-y), within the 46% ±1.5pp guidance.•

Opex ratio was 30.2% (vs 31.2% in 2Q26 and 30.9% in 3Q25), within the 31% ±2pp
guidance.
•
OPM was 16.1% (+1.1pp q-q, +0.5pp y-y).•
Net income was TWD28.2bn (+14.6% q-q, +10.8% y-y). Net margin was 16.8%
(+0.6pp q-q, -1.1pp y-y).

TWD17.92.
•
Average FX was TWD31.99/USD (2Q26: 31.60; guidance assumption: 32).•

MediaTek completed a USD3.9bn overseas convertible bond offering in 3Q, with

participation from nVidia (NVDA US, Not rated) and Alphabet (GOOGL US, Not
rated).
This strengthens partnerships with key AI infrastructure players and helps secure
capacity for critical components.
○
•
MediaTek continues to make solid progress in both data center and edge devices.
Datacenter ASIC
Current gen: Demand remains very strong. Initial shipments began in
September, and MediaTek is confident of delivering USD2.5bn of revenue in
4Q26.
The 4Q upside comes from robust customer demand. MediaTek has

worked closely with supply chain partners to secure supply, including
substrates.
■
For 2027E, the program is expected to reach the high end of the revenue
target range, about USD16bn, or around 20% of the AI accelerator ASIC
market. This is underpinned by a solid supply outlook, and MediaTek is
working to secure additional capacity to meet demand.
■
•
Next gen: Development is progressing well and is on track for HVM in 1Q28.
The chip is purpose-built for the customer’s next-generation AI

infrastructure. It delivers multiple times higher performance per watt at a
significantly lower TCO.
■
It is expected to strengthen MediaTek’s market position and contribute
substantially to revenue growth.
■

Technology: Solid progress continues in 448G SerDes, 3.5D advanced
packaging, and CPO.

and rack solutions. It connects custom xPUs to the NVLink Fusion ecosystem
and also supports UALink.

Q&A
Any changes in ASIC schedule and milestones?
The 2nd ASIC is on track and on schedule.
MediaTek is aware of market noise about the tape-out schedule. The chip has

multiple dies, and a couple have already taped out.
○
The large, more complex die will also tape out on schedule, though it takes about
three weeks longer than the others.
○
•
2028 will be another year of strong y-y growth based on the demand profile. No
details yet, as the supply chain is still being finalized.
•
EMIB-T confidence
MediaTek is working with the US IDM’s packaging process and has built a strong in-
house manufacturing team led by experienced fab veterans.

•
It is working closely with the IDM and substrate suppliers.•
Management has high confidence that yield will improve quickly once two or three key
problems are resolved.
MediaTek is working with both Taiwanese and Japanese suppliers.○
•
MediaTek is not dependent on a single supplier.
TSMC (2330 TT, Buy) remains its most important partner, and the two work
closely across the entire technology chain.
○
•
Next-next gen, TAM and contribution
MediaTek is working very closely with the customer on the next-next generation.•

The customer is aggressive in pushing performance up and TCO down.•
This will be another challenging chip, and the definition will take time to finalize.•
Management will share more when the time is right.•

### Page 4

New ASIC client
A 2nd customer is confirmed, separate from the lead customer.•
The project has kicked off, with first revenue and mass production expected in 2028.•
The customer is not from China.•
Vendor financing for LLM customers?
MediaTek will consider it if necessary and if it is the right customer.•
Returns versus capex have improved over the past six quarters. Management 
believes capex, whether from CSPs or frontier model companies, will generate 
enough revenue and cash flow. Some support may be needed during the transition 
period before cash generation stabilizes.
•
EMIB-T / HBM5 / technology capability and competition
The entry barrier is the ability to integrate multiple technologies into manufacturable, 
scalable products: advanced packaging, HBM5, 448G SerDes, and low power. It is 
not any single technology.
•
Competitors are strong, and MediaTek does not assume it is far ahead. However, it is 
confident it will win its share of the business.
•
NVLink Fusion benefits and customer interest
It is a business model with strong technical content, and it makes it much easier for 
customers to integrate their own designs.
•
The key advantage is much faster time to compute power.•
MediaTek and nVidia are in discussions with several potential customers. Feedback 
is positive, and MediaTek hopes to announce cases in the near term.
•
448G
448G SerDes has already taped out.•
448G is a must going forward. It is an N6 design with very stringent power 
consumption requirements.
•
It will not be on the critical path, and MediaTek will improve interoperability to serve a 
broader customer base.
•

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

### Page 1

Hon Hai Precision Industry

HHTD 2026: precision manufacturing, 
vertical integration, AI DC, and new EV

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 320.00 TWD | Price: 245.50 TWD

7th HHTD: precision manufacturing, vertical integration 
Hon Hai hosted its seventh Tech Day, titled "Accelerating Intelligence" on 30 Oct. Key 
highlights included its strengths in precision manufacturing, vertical integration, robotics 
and AI datacenters, as well as the launch of the Model A+ and Model K/K+ EVs. We 
expect Hon Hai, with its strong manufacturing capability and vertical integration 
advantages, to sustain its leading position as an AI server ODM. Maintain Buy. 
Doubled AI rack production to 2k racks per week currently  
Hon Hai's Chairman indicated that the company has shortened the assembly time for a 
compute tray from 2 hours to 20 minutes. This was supported by its precision 
manufacturing capability (1µm precision), automation and a cableless design co-
developed with its customer. Amid continued strong AI demand, Hon Hai has doubled its 
AI server rack production from 1,000 racks per week a year ago to 2,000 racks per week. 
AI server solution offerings across L1-L12 and CPO 
The company can now provide up to 50% of server rack components (excluding 
GPUs/CPUs), with solution offerings spanning L1 to L12. It demonstrated its high-speed 
cables/connectors, power busbars/whips, liquid cooling solutions (manifolds/cold 
plates/UQDs), compute/switch trays including the NVIDIA Spectrum-X CPO switch tray, 
server racks, as well as SuperPOD and AI datacenter solutions. 
Robotics for factory automation and new EV 
Hon Hai showcased live demonstrations of its robotics, highlighting capabilities such as 
AI-planned assembly paths and real-time sensing and tracking. For EVs, the company 
launched Model A+ and Model K/K+. Model A+ features L4 readiness, four-wheel drive 
and a spacious cabin. Model K converts from a truck into a van with a canopy box, while 
Model K+ features a more stylish design to serve the Japanese and European markets.

Company Sector

Company Description 
Hon Hai is the world's largest EMS provider, with four key 
business segments - Smart Consumer Electronics, Cloud & 
Networking, Computing, and Components & Others. Hon 
Hai supplies components, final assembly, and global 
logistics for global tech giants, including Apple, Microsoft, 
Google, HP, Dell, Cisco, and Sony. Hon Hai has 
R&D/production sites globally, including Taiwan, China, 
India, Japan, Vietnam, Malaysia, Czech Republic, Mexico, and 
the US. The company was established in Taiwan in 1974.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Hon Hai as its gradual moves into 
high margin/value-added server/components/semi 
business, stronger position in AI server with rising sales, 
solid balance sheet, and attractive valuation among Greater 
China AI server players.

Price objective basis & risk 
Hon Hai Precision Industry (HNHAF) 
We set our PO at NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS), at around 3SD above historical P/E 
average since 2007. We think the valuation is justified as HH's leading position in 
EMS/ODM business, improving iPhone momentum with replacement cycle, rising sales 
from AI business, more diversified global footprint than peers to mitigate 
tariff/geopolitical risk, and improving balance sheet with higher cash dividend payout.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker momentum from Apple business (iPhone, iPad 
and Apple Watch), (2) increasing competition in EMS business from peers, (3) slower 
sales/earnings delivery in auto/EV business, and (4) weaker market demand or lower 
shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better-than-expected yield rate and demand for iPhone, 
iPad and Apple Watch business, (2) further automation adoption to reduce labor costs 
and improve efficiency, (3) faster sales/earnings delivery from auto/EV business, and (4) 
stronger market demand or higher shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price

### Page 1

M
Idea
Axion CPU demand remains strong and on track: We noted  in a prior report  that
demand for Google's Axion CPUs, which GUC helps design, remains robust.
However, we now see further upside in both 2027 and 2028 from our previous
forecast. Also, some investors have questioned whether the Axion3 schedule has
been pushed out. Our  recent supply chain checks suggest that Axion3 has already
reached wafer-in and is on track for wafer-out in mid-2027. Axion CPU could reach 4-

5mn units of  Axion2 equivalent CPU in 2027, given that Axion3 includes two
computing dies but also adopts TSMC 3nm process. We now forecast volume at
4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3 in 2027, then 3mn units of Axion3 in
2028. We project ASP at US$700 and US$1400, respectively.
We also see upside surprise from Cobalt CPU: We believe the CPU demand upside
is coming not just from Axion but also Microsoft's Cobalt CPU. GUC continues to
get more front-end wafer support from TSMC; given strong CPU demand, we now

forecast >1mn units of Cobalt 200 in 2027 with ASP of ~US$700. Our industry
checks also suggest that Cobalt 300 is on track for mass production in 2028, which
might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die
CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be ~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
3Q26 revenue beat from CPU customers' demand pull-forward and new
customer: The company posted its  3Q26 results: revenue beat our estimate

strongly (by 23%);  the upside comes mostly from the largest CPU customer's pulling
demand forward  (50-55% of 3Q26 total revenue) and also the new US AI customer,
which has adopted the 5nm process and SoW (system-on-wafer) at TSMC. In 4Q26,
we expect a major Chinese auto company to start to contribute revenue. With the
ongoing robust CPU demand, we now expect 4Q26 revenue to be up 20% QoQ. See
Exhibit 3  for our 3Q26 earnings review.
Reiterate OW and raise price target to NT$8600: This 37% increase mostly

reflects the upside we have seen from CPU demand. However, aside from CPU
demand, we also see strong revenue from Tesla, Meta, Horizon, etc.   in 2028. Thus,
we expect 2025-28 revenue CAGR to reach 120%. Our new price target implies P/E
of 52x for 2027e and 27x for 2028e, which we think is justified by its strong revenue
growth.

### Page 2

Idea
2
Strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
Axion CPU should be the largest customer for GUC in 2026-2028: We therefore now
forecast only 1.5mn units of Google Axion2 CPU in 2026, given the demand pull-in. By
assuming chip ASP at ~US$700, we now see 2026 Google CPU contributing slightly more
than US$1000mn.
For 2027, we have been seeing strong rises in demand expectations for Axion CPU,
including  the ramp of the next-generation Axion3. We now expect Axion CPU revenue of

close to US$3.5bn, including 4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3. In addition,
we also expect the chip ASP of Axion3 to double from Axion2 given the increase of 3nm
computing dies from one to two.
For 2028, we now forecast  3mn units of Axion3, slightly lower than Axion2's annual run
rate of 4mn units, mostly also driven by the doubling of computing dies.
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin for Axion CPU – likely around 10% –
because this is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design

support (e.g., silicon interposer and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for
the ASIC designs. We also expect margin for Axion3 to be slightly lower than for Axion2,
given higher chip ASP or larger project size.
Cobalt demand could  also grow 3-4x in 2027: GUC also helps with Microsoft's Cobalt
200/300 CPU. We now forecast at least US$1bn of GUC revenue contribution from
Cobalt 200 and Maia 200 in 2027. Also, our industry checks also suggest that Cobalt 300

is on track for mass production in 2028, which might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By
assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be
~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
Meta ASIC (MTIA 600): In our AI supply chain tracker, we noted our belief that GUC
could win one of the Meta ASIC projects, led by the Rivos team, and we now think it might
be MTIA 600, based on supply chain checks. The AI ASIC targets tape-out in 1H27, with

potential CoWoS out in 2028.
Note: these forecasts are from Asia Pacific research and incorporate supply chain and
industry checks.

4
Estimate revision summary
We raise   our EPS estimates 17% for 2026, 18% for 2027, and 50% for 2028: Our
increases come mostly from the strength in CPU as well as other AI projects' contribution,
including Meta ASIC, US design house adopting SoW, etc. With turnkey revenue share
seen increasing in both 2027 and 2028, we now assume that gross margin will fall from
21.4% in 2026 to 15.4% in 2028. However, we expect firmer operating margin this year,

thanks to a policy change regarding employee bonus; although this assumption dips in
2027, it rebounds in 2028 because of larger revenue scale with better operating leverage.
The same trend is visible in  net margin.

Price target rises 37%, to NT$8,600
This reflects our 2027-28 estimate revisions. Although we are cutting our terminal growth
rate assumption from 5% to 4.5% because of margin dilution, our other  key valuation
assumptions are unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium
of 6%, and a risk-free rate of 2%), and intermediate growth rate  of 14.5%.
Our bull and bear case values rise by similar magnitudes, to   NT$11,680  and NT$5,300,

respectively.

Relative valuation
Our price target of NT$8,600 implies 52x our 2027 EPS estimate, around +1 SD from
GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market

will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term, and therefore believe
that further re-rating would be justified with project wins likely to come. Despite slower
recovery of non-AI business, we continue to appreciate GUC’s growing exposure to cloud
AI semis (including AI accelerators, AI server CPU, and BMC chips).

NT$11,680.00
71x 2027e EPS
Multiple leading-edge NRE projects
successfully translate into volume
production with healthy margin proﬁles.

Many AI chipsets or other projects start to
proliferate with substantial end demand,
with customers' interest in migrating to
more advanced nodes. Networking switch
demand is stronger than expected, while
SSD controller project contributions are
sustainable in 2026. 3/5nm turnkey revenue
growth is stronger than expected in 2026
and beyond.
NT$8,600.00
52x 2027e EPS
Networking switch turnkey projects volume
remains stable. A few new leading-edge
turnkey projects start to show decent

shipments. TSMC's leading-edge tape-out
numbers increase steadily, while some
"turnkey 2/3" (TK2 and TK3) projects
contribute to NRE and turnkey revenue. CSP
(AI) revenue contribution should be the
major growth driver for 2026 and 2027, as
well as the auto chip and SSD controller
projects.
NT$5,300.00
32x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and other projects lack concrete
end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs.
TSMC's leading-edge tape-out numbers
decrease or some customers turn directly to

TSMC. BMC and SSD project losses arise
from customers' in-sourcing. Fiercer-than-
expected talent competition leads to higher
opex to retain R&D engineers. High volume
turnkey projects carry lower-than-expected
gross margin.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
3
AI infrastructure demand, supply constraints and 
engineering productivity are key for growth outlook
Despite rising concerns over data center power availability, infrastructure 
deployment delays and potential AI investment moderation, management reported 
no meaningful slowdown in customer demand or changes in development 
schedules. Based on discussions with major customers, Alchip expects strong 
demand extending through 2027–2029, with next-generation AI accelerator 
volumes potentially exceeding current-generation levels substantially. 
Management cautioned that shipment delays could arise from supply-side 
constraints, particularly PCB, substrates, passive components and advanced 
semiconductor capacity, rather than weakening end demand. More importantly,  
large AI accelerator projects generally receive priority allocation, leaving smaller 
customers and traditional applications more exposed to shortages. Meanwhile, AI 
adoption is materially improving Alchip’s engineering productivity, particularly in 
RTL coding, verification, design iteration and analysis of implementation reports. 
Alchip’s CPU and networking projects are progressing on schedule, but 
management believes advanced AI accelerator designs offer substantially greater 
engineering differentiation and revenue opportunities than conventional CPU 
projects.
Looking into 2028, the key limitation could shift from customer demand to Alchip’s 
ability to finance and support larger wafer procurement commitments. 
Consequently, working-capital requirements, customer payment terms and the 
potential need for additional financing will become increasingly important factors 
in determining the company’s achievable revenue scale and profitability. 
We lift our 2027 /2028 earnings projection for Alchip by 14% and 13%, reflecting 
stronger order allocation into next two years. We are maintaining our Buy on the 
stock but lowering our TP to NT$6,000, with lower PER at 27x of 2027 EPS (from 
35x previously), reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty, in the low end of its 
historical PER.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,000 is based on 27x 2027E EPS. 27x target
PER is lower than its historical 3-year PER range, which we see as justified on
account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with its key CSP
client, reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty but with better project cycle
and strong growth momentum resuming next year. Our target price equates
to 2026/27E P/B of 6x/5x.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

壹、訪談／法說內容 
重點摘要: 
 記憶體漲價帶動獲利： 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% ，毛 利 率77.44%；預 估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0% ，稅後 EPS 39.50 元。  
 成熟記憶體供給吃緊： DDR2 、DDR3 合計營收占比逾五成，
受惠同業減供及客戶轉單，加上高階、商務應用需求暢旺，支
撐產品價格維持高檔。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 102.33 ／159.38 元：受惠合約
價格調升及低基期，預估全年營收分別為 672.85 ／1,019.90
億元， YoY +361.6% ／+51.6% 。

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升  
晶豪科主要從事記憶體 IC 設計，產品涵蓋 DRAM、Flash、MCP 、
eMCP 及 eMMC ，今年記憶體產品營收比重約 95% 。受惠記憶體
供給吃緊、合約價格逐季調升， 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% 、YoY +519.22% ；毛利率 77.44% ，較上季增加 8.74 個
百分點；營業利益 131.50 億元， QoQ +83.33% ，營業利益率
59.37% ； 稅後純益107.59 億元 ，QoQ +82.20% ，稅 後EPS 36.79
元。營收及獲利成長主要來自產品價格上漲，出貨量受限於合作
晶圓廠產能，增幅相對有限。

二、 DDR2 、DDR3 營收占比五成，供給收縮支撐價格  
隨記憶體大廠將資源轉向 DDR4 、DDR5 及 HBM ，成熟記憶體供
給減少，缺貨情況逐步擴及 DDR3 、DDR2 及 Flash 。公司表示，
國內同業停止或減少供應 DDR2 、DDR3 ，帶動客戶轉單，第三季
兩項產品合計營收占比已超過 50% 。需求方面， PC、NB 及手機
因應 AI 應用提高記憶體搭載容量，加上 AI 基礎架構、周邊設備
與邊緣運算需求增加，有助帶動非 HBM 記憶體需求。儘管部分消
費性客戶因價格漲幅較大而觀望，高階及商務應用拉貨仍暢旺，
支撐公司接單表現。

三、 4Q26 預估營收季增 8%，aiPIM 布局邊緣 AI 應用  
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格將高於第三季，出貨量則預期與
第三季相近。考量合約價格續升，預估 4Q26 營收 239.22 億元，
QoQ 8.0% ；毛利率 78.44% ，營業利益 144.40 億元，稅後純益
投資評等：買進

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 320 元 
考量 AI 趨勢下帶動原廠將產能轉做 HBM，以 及DDR 4 轉入 DDR 
5，帶動利基型記憶體市場正向效益，記憶體供不應求的現象持
續，帶動價格持續大幅上升，使晶豪科營收獲利自 4Q25 開始明
顯跳升， 2027 年全年供需缺口無解，價格上升趨勢趨緩但仍有上
行空間，帶動晶豪科獲利維持高檔，因此，投資建議買進，目標
價 320 元(2X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
晶豪科 (3006) 成立於 1998 年 ， 公司主要以提供各類型記憶體產品
及技術為主。目前記憶體佔營收比重約 90% ，Audio 佔 10% ，其
中特定型 DRAM 產品廣泛應用於 PC 週邊 、 資訊家電 、 及消費性、
通訊等系統。公司已成功建立各種容量及介面規格的特定型
DRAM 產品線 (包括 SDRAM 、DDR I/II/III 及 PSRAM 、低耗電之
Mobile DRAM 等)；在快閃記憶體方面，亦已完成多種容量及介面
類型的 NOR Flash 及 NAND Flash 的開發，可滿足各種特定應用
系統的需求。另外，由於系統級封裝 (SiP) 之需求快速增加，晶豪
科技也已經成功開發上述各項產品所需之「良品晶粒」
(Known-Good-Die;KGD) 產品及多晶片模組封裝 (MCP) 的解決方
案，以滿足客戶的各類需求。尤於晶豪科記憶體產品大部份以利
基型記憶體為主，受標準型記憶體價格波動干擾程度相對較低，
有助維持穩定毛利率。

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3

二.營運概況及展望

1、IoT 應用持續成長，為重要成長動能 
5G 於 2H19 正式商轉，隨著基礎建設逐漸完善，連網速度及穩定
度成熟， 2021 年進入大幅應用階段，除了 5G 智慧型手機需求量
將從 2020 年的 2～2.5 億支倍數成長至 4.5 ～5 億支之外，因 5G
高頻、高速、廣連結的特色，連網速度將較 4G 時代大幅提升 10
～100 倍，將推升物聯網 (IoT) 應用快速成長，預估 5G 同時連網
裝置可達 600 ～800 億個，較 4G 時代的 100 億個大幅成長。看好
未來物聯網市場將蓬勃發展 ， 晶豪科於2020 年新成立物聯網解決
方案部門，提供客戶 RF、Wireless 的 SoC IC 及模組提供低功率
網際網路進行裝置連結，目前使用 90nm 製程節點，未來將進入
到 55nm 。2020 年新成立的物聯網解決方案部門站營收比重僅
<0.5% ，但因全球物聯網發展將因 5G 建設而蓬勃發展，預估繼
2022 年逆勢成長後， 2023 年可望延續此一成長動能。

IOT 為晶豪科近年延伸觸角的重要產品 。 IOT 將建構於 5G 佈建的
完整性， 2020 年為 5G 建置元年， 2021 年將加速建置，加速 IOT
產業發展。由於大部份 IOT 產品僅需要 DDR 3 規格即可，且全球
主要記憶體廠均優先佈局以 Server 使用的 DDR 5 為主，因此，
預估未來 DDR 3 將保持穩定的需求量。晶豪科將以無線模組切入
中國智慧電表領域，及其他各類相關 IOT 產品，為未來營運成長
重要動能。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升 
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格高於第三季，出貨量則預期與第
三季相近 。 考量合約價格續升 ， 預估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0%；毛 利 率78.44%， 營業利益144.40 億元 ， 稅後純益117.76
億元， EPS 39.50 元。惟晶圓代工及封裝測試成本逐步增加，後
續仍須關注售價調升與成本上漲的相對幅度。新產品方面，公司
針對邊緣 AI 運算開發 aiPIM 加速器記憶體，第三季已完成
tape-out ，後續關注晶片驗證、客戶導入及量產進度，短期營運仍
以既有記憶體產品為主要動能。。

億元，毛利率 72.75% ；營業利益 370.17 億元，稅後純益 305.07
億元，稅後 EPS 102.33 元。展望 2027 年，受惠成熟記憶體供給

2026/10/30 投資速報

創意 (3443.TT) 
持續優於預期，CPU 配比提升將成為趨勢

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對創意的投資建議維持強力買進，係因:(1)3Q26 營收、毛利
率、營業利益率皆優於預期，(2)4Q26 雖因為 ECB 將送件未給財測，
但客戶對 CPU 需求來看仍持續上修 ，(3)北美車用晶片有望在 2Q27 初
導入 ， 中國車用客戶晶片市佔率持續提升 ， 且持續拓展海外客戶 ，27-
28 年貢獻幅度大幅跳升 ，(4)晶片大廠持續上修 CPU 與加速器的配適
性，更有效率的使用 GPU 之效能，(5)不僅 200 系列晶片，CSP 客戶
的 300 系列晶片創意也有所著墨。綜合以上，上修創意 26 年 EPS 預
估為 63.3 元(原估 54.76 元，+125%YOY)，同步上修 27 年 EPS 預估
為 245.9 元(原估 214.5 元，+288.5%YOY)， 及上修28 年EPS 為 467.7
元(原估 392.6 元，+95.9%YOY，以股本膨脹 3%計算)。考量 27 年下
半年創意的成長動能更加明確上修 ， 且費用控管十分優異 ， 客戶從雲
端 AI 延伸至終端 AI，CPU、加速器、自駕晶片及 CPO 皆有著墨，故
對創意看法更加樂觀，故先前 8600 元目前價到價後，仍維持強力買
進投資建議，目標價調升至 9850 元(27 年 EPS*40XPER OR 28 年
EPS*21XPER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 營收大幅優於預期，OPM 更加優化：  
創意 3Q26 營收達到創新高且優於上修後的預期的 187.5 億
元，成長35%QOQ，成長117.7%YOY，其中量產營收達到166.7
億元，成長44%QOQ，推估來自於CPU 的貢獻較大，且在2H26
創意也有新增一些產能支援 ， 且NRE 也達到預估的區間上緣 ，
僅衰退 11%QOQ。 毛利率達到20.59%， 優於研究部預期的19%，
更優於市場預估的 19-20%，推估主要是出貨的晶圓良率更加
提升所致。費用控制非常良好，3Q26 費用僅 13.09 億元，低
於 2Q26 的 13.18 億元，但營收卻成長35%QOQ，故使得OPM 提
升至 13.6%，大幅優於先前預估的11%。稅後淨利達到創新高
的 22.73 億元(原估 16.02 億元)，成長 46.1%QOQ，成長
162.1%YOY，EPS 16.96 元(原估 11.96 元)。

2. 因 ECB 將送件，故無 4Q26 財測，但仍正向看待：  
因為創意的 ECB 即將送件，故此次財報後無法給出財測，但
研究部仍認為 4Q26 創意營運動能將上修，主要係因先前在上
半年所提到的投片保有量外，仍有多爭取的 WAFER，從創意
3Q26 每個月的量產營收就可以觀察到持續增加，單月量產營
收已經提升至 65 億元，預估 4Q26 將能持續穩定攀升。另外
NRE 方面，CSP 下一代 CPU 將預估在 4Q26 TAPE OUT，NRE 預

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

估將翻倍成長 。 綜合以上 ，因為量產營收持續優於預期， 故上
修 4Q26 營收為 244.2 億元(原估 236 億元)，成長30.2%QOQ，
成長 97%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為續創新高的 30.1 億元(原
估 25.35 億元)，成長32.5%QOQ，成長159.6%YOY，EPS 22.46
元(原估 18.92 元)。

3. CPU在Agentic AI世代重要性持續提升： 
根據INTEL的資料中心與人工智慧白皮書顯示，CPU 容量不足
可能造成編排卡頓、工具執行延遲、驗證循環變慢，導致GPU
在等待控制平面 （代理編排） 處理完成時利用率不足 。 若要將
代理型工作負載最佳化，就必須增加 CPU 數量，作為控制與
編排層，在兼顧 新式 AI 工作流程分散且多步驟本質的同
時，將資料高效提供給加速器。故根據INTEL實測顯示，在模
型大小為8B規模時 ， 建議加速器與CPU配比為1.4:1， 模型大小
擴大到70B時，建議配比為1:1。故在AMD簡報也顯示，2025-
2030年CPU產值規模將從260億美元提升至2030年的2200億美
元，CAGR達到50%。創意的CSP客戶所ASIC走到第八代後，CPU
與TPU的配置從先前1:4確實提升至1:2，並且全數從先前的
X86 BASE更改成自家研發的ARM BASE CPU。而CSP客戶第八代
產品放量時間點將落在4Q26末，2027年完整貢獻 ， 故從創意量
產營收看出 ， 因為配比提升CSP客戶對CPU需求持續增加 ， 故上
修CPU26年貢獻創意營收從12億美元上修至13-15億美元。在
27年方面，有鑑於客戶對於加速器及CPU的配飾性相對於第七
代提升一倍，加上下一代CPU將在4Q26 TAPE OUT，有望在2H27
開始貢獻。因為二代產品單價提升，故上修CPU貢獻營收程度
從35億美元上修35-40億美元。 
CSP二代CPU將有望在28年完整貢獻，ASP提升幅度較大，且三
代CPU目前已經開始著手研發 ， 故研究部認為 ，CPU對加速器的
配置開始往1:1走後，創意目前在客戶需求延續性強，且持續
有拿到次世代案件下，28年CPU貢獻幅度將達到50%YOY增長。

圖一、INTEL 資料中心白皮書內容

資料來源：INTEL，統一研究部整理

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

圖二、AMD 對 SERVER CPU 產值的預估

資料來源：AMD，統一研究部整理

車用將成為 27 年開始的新成長支柱 
(1)北美客戶： 
根據供應鏈訪查 ， 北美車用客戶晶片量產時間點將落在2Q26，
創意目前在 5 代晶片進度仍優於競爭對手，故以目前規劃的
投片量來看 ， 研究部維持先前預估貢獻約12 億美金營收不變 。
但根據北美客戶法說，該客戶希望將5 代晶片在 2027 年 4 月
導入機器人使用 ， 故研究部目前僅估算車用的部分 ， 尚未估計
機器人，若該客戶順利量產機器人，則貢獻創意營收有望上
修。 
(2)中國客戶： 
以中國客戶自行揭露的資料來看，全系列征程系列晶片出貨
套數已經高達 1000 萬套，在 26 年 9 月，該客戶的 CEO 在社
群媒體上表示，公司智駕晶片出貨量已達 1500 萬套。另外客
戶給出 26 年征程系列晶片將出貨 500 萬套，27 年將更達到
700 萬套的目標。先前預估 27 年以客戶出貨目標 700 萬套的
50%，350 萬套第 7 代征程晶片做估計，貢獻營收將達到 15-
20 億美元，此預估沒有變化，靜待客戶完成年報公告後再行
調整。

圖三、中國自駕車晶片公司 CEO 微博

資料來源：WEIBO，統一研究部整理

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

兩大車用客戶對創意來說 ，27年僅是開端 ， 首先北美客戶27年
才會導入5代晶片，且是機器人及無人計程車皆會導入，附帶
一提，先前展示的飛天跑車也是搭載5代晶片，故認為在客戶
量產機器人及無人計程車後，晶片需求在 28年將會超過翻倍
成長 。 另外在中國客戶方面， 除了本土市場外 ， 該公司積極拓
展海外市場，7代晶片LIFECYCLE有望比6代更久，28年目前也
以翻倍的狀況預估。

4. CSP客戶三代晶片仍有著墨機會： 
北美另一雲端客戶在10月初表示，200系列加速器已經實現量
產，該客戶已經在各地區DATA CENTER 部屬200系列晶片，而
200系列晶片後段為創意打造，推估LIFE CYCLE出貨量為數十
萬顆 ， 貢獻營收約3-5億美元 。 而在300系列方面 ， 先前市場傳
出主要供應商為美國晶片同業 ， 後來研究部訪查 ， 創意在27年
亦有BOOKING 2nm產品，而300系列晶片由N2P節點打造，故推
估創意亦有部分300系列晶片相關生意，故仍有營收貢獻。而
目前推估400系列晶片有望重回創意，挹注 28年後的營運動
能。

5. 26、27年上修預估，28年持續高度成長： 
創意3Q26營收已經大幅優於預期 ， 加上毛利率擊敗市場預期 ，
另外費用控管得宜 ， 使得3Q26 OPM不減反增 ， 另外4Q26雖然未
給財測，但在產能有所支持加上CPU需求持續推升下，營收也
略有上修，故上修創意26年營收預估為685.2億元(原估648.6
億元)，成長100.7%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為84.8億元(原估
73.28億元)， 成長125%YOY，EPS上修預估為63.3元(原估54.76
元)。 展望27年，CPU貢獻性持續上修 ， 另外也維持車用客戶的
預估值，另外本來就有CSP客戶的200系列加速器開始大幅量
產，300系列目前有所著墨，故上修27年營收預估為2458.5億
元(原估2328億元)，成長258.8%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為
329.5億元(原估287.5億元)， 成長288.5%YOY，EPS上修預估為
245.9元(原估214.5元)。最後，雖然目前28年投片等尚未確
定 ， 不過創意在CSP二代CPU、 中國及北美車用晶片 ， 接續世代
晶片皆有持續獲得新世代案件 ， 另外在CSP ASIC方面 ， 目前有
將從整合封裝能力開始切入 ，CSP 300/400系列晶片皆有斬獲 ，
故研究 部粗估28年創意營收將上修預估為 5145.8億元(原估
4627.5億元)，成長109.3%YOY，稅後淨利預估上修為645.5億
元(原估542億元)，成長95.9%YOY，EPS預估為467.7元(原估
392.6元，以膨脹後3%股本計算)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：富世達成立於民國 90 年，母公司為上市公司奇鋐(3017 TT)，公司提供
各類金屬沖壓設計與製造，主要產品為摺疊手機、筆記型電腦、平板電腦之轉軸零
件，近年跨入伺服器領域。3Q26 營收比重：摺疊手機鉸鍊約佔 15.5%，SERVER
相關約佔 81.3%。  
3Q26 本業略優於預期：3Q26 營收 45.02 億(+20.4%QoQ、+26.2%YoY)，受惠
於產品組合、製程改善與自動化導入，3Q26 毛利率達 38%，表現優於預期，營業
利益 14.6 億元，稅後 EPS 18.12 元。 
4Q26 展望持續正向：展望 4Q26 雖然摺疊手機部分，因為記憶體缺貨，終端產品
需求不佳等因素出貨不如預期，但隨著 NVIDIA VR200 與各大專案陸續放量，公
司對整體營運持續維持正向看法 ， 預期伺服器相關出貨將持續增加且營收佔比預期
將維持在高檔； 同時受惠於高毛利的伺服器產品比重提升、製程持續優化與自動化
導入，整體毛利率可望獲得持續改善。預估4Q26 營收 49.66 億元(+10.3%QoQ，
+24.88%YoY)，毛利率 38.15%，稅後 EPS 19.65 元。 
2027 年~2028 年成長動能不虞匱乏：展望 2027 年主要成長動能包括：(1) 預期
對 AWS 的出貨將於 1Q27 開始明顯增溫，Meta、Google、微軟等 CSP 大廠皆於
其 AI 伺服器導入水冷方案 ， 出貨量可望於2027 年放大且貢獻全年營收 ；(2) VR200
已開始出貨，除了浮動接頭持續出貨外，水冷接頭應用由 switch tray 延伸至
computer tray 與 manifold，且營收貢獻延續至 2027 年全年。(3) Vera Rubin 
Ultra swith tray 內 QD 用量由 4 顆變 8 顆，computer tray 相關有 10 顆 MQD
改為 ZQD 有利 ASP 提升(預期較目前 ASP 高一倍以上)，研究處評估整體 Vera 
Rubin ultra 水冷頭產值將較 Vera Rubin 進一步提升 50%以上，順利的將可於
2H27 開始貢獻營收，且貢獻度延伸至 2028 年。 
持續擴充產能：3Q2Q 水冷快接頭產能利用率達 80%~90%，而新產能目前已經
順利開出，產能由先前的3KK 擴增至 4.5KK，預期 2027 年仍將有擴產規劃；滑軌
產能目前維持在先前規劃的 350K 套的水準 ， 後續將視專案認證進度動態規劃產能
擴充。 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 174 億元(+40%YoY)，毛利率 35.8%，稅後 EPS 63.51
元。(前次預估 2026 稅後 EPS 61.86 元)。 預估2027 年營收 245 億元(+41%YoY)，

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 269: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27 년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3 배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 5 9%, 4 8%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다.

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
79

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

[투자등급 비율 2026-09-30 기준]

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

## Document 303: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

### Page 1

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  硬體‧美國

Flex 
(FLEX.O/ FLEX US)

預期資料中心電源需求為獲利加速成長之主要動能 
重要訊息 
Flex FY2Q27 財報與 FY3Q27 財測均優於市場共識 ，公司上修 FY2027 資料
中心年增率自 70%至 80%，收盤股價上漲約 12%。 
評論及分析  
FY2Q27 資料中心電源需求升溫， EPS 年增 49%至 1.18 美元並優於共識。
Flex FY2Q27 (2026 年 7–9 月)營收年增 25%、季增 7%至 84.9 億美元，主
要來自資料中心電源 需求延續 ；受惠於電源產品營利率 年增 5.5ppt s 至約
16.0%、帶動產品組合轉佳， Flex FY2Q27  EPS 年增 49%、季增 18%至
1.18 美元，大致符合凱基原預估、優於市場共識 12%。 
FY3Q27 資料中心成長性可望優於 FY2Q27，預估 EPS 年增 52%至 1.32 美
元。Flex 已於 10 月完成對 EPC Power 的收購，該公司可望貢獻單季營收約
2 億美元，並有助於 800V 架構及固態變壓器( SST)的技術能力。FY 3Q27 隨
Hyperscalers 與 Neo clouds 等客戶需求延續，凱基預估 Flex 營收年增
33%、季增 11%至 93.9 億美元，其中資料中心業務之營收年增率預期達
115%，其餘產品線則約 10%上下；受惠營利率較佳的資料中心業務貢獻提
升，FY3Q27  EPS 預估年增 52%、季增 11%至 1.32 美元。 
凱基預估 Flex FY2027/FY2028  EPS 為 5.24/8.80 美元。基於電源產品接單
成長性優於預期以及併購 EPC Power 也將挹注營收，Flex 上修 FY2027 資料
中心營收年增率 自 70%至 80%，FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，隱含
FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上修 約 6% 、營收占比預期達
33%/45%。基於 Flex 上修展望，凱基 將 Flex FY2027/ FY2028 EPS 自原預估
之 4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%。 
投資建議  
凱基維持「增加持股」評等，目標價上修至 176 美元 (20x FY2028F EPS) 。 
投資風險  
關稅戰惡化導致客戶下單意願降低； CPI 業務營運成長態勢不如預期。

### Page 2

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Flex 資料中心營收占比提升至 30%。Flex 三大產品線為 ITS (包含網通與智慧
生活，FY 2Q27 營收占比 39%；營利率 5.5%)、RMS ( 包含工業、汽車與醫
療，FY1Q27 營收占比 31%；營利率 6.4%)、CPI ( 資料中心業務，FY1Q27
營收占比 30%；營利率 10.7%)。各項業務之應用主要包含:  (1) 工業產品涵
蓋資本設備、工業裝置、再生能源、電源與電網邊緣等解決方案； (2) 車用
產品主要提供下一代移動方案、自駕、連網化 、電動化與智慧科技 ；(3) 健
康解決方案包含醫療裝置、醫療設備與藥物輸送系統； (4) 智慧生活包含消
費性電子、家電、消費性包裝、掃地機器人、微型移動裝置及音訊產品； (5) 
網通領域涵蓋通訊基礎設施與企業網路傳輸； (6) 資料中心業務 包含電源、
散熱、系統整合等產品與服務。   
Flex 目前的發展策略是結合 EMS 與產品的服務策略以提升接單能力，公司
表示自身是全球唯一具有提供從資料中心晶片到電網能力的供應商，除了可
提供嵌入式電源 (embedded power) 產品、管理伺服器 GPU 的電源，亦可
滿足客戶對資料中心內所有設施的電力與散熱需求，提供完整的客製化服
務，帶動公司資料中心營收占比自前一季之 28%提升至 FY2Q27 之 30%。

圖 1: Flex CPI部門為近幾季主要成長動能，營收占比逐步提升至30%

Flex FY2027/ FY2028 資料中心營收與獲利成長性可望優於產業平均 。Flex
計劃將 CPI 部門分拆成一家新的上市公司 Axiom，維持在 CY1Q27 完成分拆
的進度不變， Flex 自身將專注於醫療保健、機器人、自動化倉儲和網通等產
業，兩家公司可更聚焦 於彼此的業務並提升資金運用效率。公司 提及自身在
資料中心電源布局相對完整，且產品價格亦相對具有競爭優勢下，使公司在
電源產品接單成長性優於產業平均； 隨包含 Google 在內的 Hyperscalers 、
Colocations 及 Neo-clouds 等客戶對嵌入式電源與 系統級解決方案的需求升
溫，公司在此次會議將 FY2027 資料中心營收年增率自 70%上修至 80%、
FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，皆較 FY2026 資料中心業務營收年增 38%
至 66 億美元加速成長 ，並隱含 FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上 修約
6%、營收占比預期達 33%/45%。

### Page 3

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

獲利性部分， 受惠於電源產品營利率較佳、 FY2Q27 年增 5.5ppts 至約
16.0%，優於資料中心業務的平均營利率約 11%以及公司整體平均營利率
約 7%，公司上修 FY2027 資料中心業務之營利率約 1ppt 至 11.6%。凱基
預估隨資料中心營收貢獻提升， FY2027/FY2028 營 利占比分別達
49%/65%，為公司主要獲利動能。  
至於資料中心以外之 其餘應用，預期營收年增率 維持約 10%上下之水準。
目前歐洲工業需求略 有回溫，營收年增率可望維持約 10%上下；汽車產品
線則因目前全球汽車銷售量已逐步回升至過往 9,000 萬輛的正常水準，
FY2027 /FY2028 產品線成長性預期落在 5%以內；醫療設備則因客戶先前在
Covid -19 時期堆積的庫存水位過高，目前庫存去化雖逐步落底，但 需求端仍
相對平緩；網通領域則預期隨庫存去化力道放緩， FY2027 產品線營收將逐
步回溫，預期年增率 約 10%；消費性電子則因記憶體漲價、品牌廠將成本
轉嫁給消費者的影響， FY202 7 營收預期年減約 5-10%。 
Flex 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價上修至 176 美元。基於 Flex 上修資料
中心之營收與營 利率展望，凱基將 Flex FY2027/FY2028 EPS 自原預估之
4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%；相較市場共識
FY202 7/FY2028  EPS 預估為 4.48/6.82 美元，股價交易於約 12/14 倍之
FY2028 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Flex 過往五年本益比多落於 5-20 倍之間，考量 (1) 公司在資料中心領域掌握
從晶片、液冷散熱、BBU 到電網的供應能力 ，布局相對多數同業更全面 ；(2) 
公司持續提升在 CSP 廠供應鏈之接單量，有助 FY2027 /FY2028 營收與獲利
加速成長； (3) 基於資料中心產品線營利率可望逐步提升至 10-15%、顯著
優於公司過往產品之 5%水準，且在獲利占比逐步提升至 50%以上的帶動
下，有助公司評價向散熱與電源同業靠攏。綜合上述 原因，評估 Flex 本益比
可望回升至區間 上緣之 20 倍上下浮動 ；基於 Flex 當前評價偏低 ，故維持
「增加持股」之投資評等，目標價隨 EPS 預估上修而調整至 176 美元 (20x 
FY202 8F EPS)。

營收與獲利主要動能。公司 三大產品線為 ITS ( 包含網通與智慧
生活)、RMS (包含工業、汽車與醫療 )、CPI (資料中心業務，包
含電源、雲端與散熱等 )，營收占比分別為 40%、 37%、
24%。FY2026 資料中心營收已達 66 億美元，營收比重約
24%。

### Page 7

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

## Document 393: Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer

Source PDF: all report pdf/Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer
Date:    6 hours ago
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Gridlock Won & Markets Cheer
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 4 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. Why Wall Street Is Cheering the Blue Sweep
Ahead of Tuesday's midterm elections, we warned that a Democratic
sweep of Congress could deliver a cold shower to Wall Street. It still could,
but it didn't do so today. Democrats captured both chambers of Congress,
flipping seven Republican-held Senate seats, including historic victories in
Texas, Kansas, and South Carolina. Financial markets put a positive spin
on the blue wave.
Oil prices fell on hopes that the US and Iran are more likely now to resolve
their war diplomatically. If so, then the outlook for inflation will improve. So
the Fed might not have to raise the federal funds rate as much. The

### Page 2

federal deficit might not widen further if Democrats refuse to increase
defense spending, while President Donald Trump vetoes their attempts to
provide Medicare for all. If Democrats slow the AI buildout, hyperscalers'
profits and free cash flow will increase. Fewer AI-related bond issues
would also help keep a lid on bond yields.
Initially, Nasdaq-100 futures plunged as much as 2.4% overnight, while
S&P 500 futures fell 1.4%. But the selloff reversed after Trump struck a

surprisingly conciliatory tone, congratulating Democrats and saying he
was willing to work with them.
That is when the cheer on Wall Street started. The Dow surged 3.0%
today, the strongest post-midterm rally since 1982. The S&P 500 jumped
1.6%, its best session in three months. The Nasdaq-100 advanced 0.9%.
The relief rally extended beyond equities. The 10-year Treasury yield fell
to 4.90%, back in what we have called the "old normal" range of
4.00%-5.00% (chart).

The dollar index (DXY) fell more than 1.5%, its biggest decline since
September. Gold rallied back above $4,000 an ounce. It might be finally
bottoming.

### Page 3

de-escalation in the Middle East
Brent crude plunged 8.7% to $94.62 a barrel amid hopes of Middle East
de-escalation. The nearby futures price of gasoline plunged to $2.73
(chart).
Investors may be warming up to the idea that some constraints on the AI
buildout could be healthy, as we have been arguing recently. Democratic
victories in key data center states, including Texas and Georgia, raise the
prospect of tighter construction restrictions amid concerns about electricity

and water consumption.
Hyperscalers rallied today, with Amazon and Microsoft gaining more than
6%, as capital discipline could ease concerns about AI investment returns
and improve free cash flow (chart). AI infrastructure providers struggled,

### Page 4

however. The PHLX Semiconductor Index fell 3.1% amid fears of weaker
demand from a slower buildout. We may be entering an AI Goldilocks
zone, with enough restraint to take some air out of the bubble without
derailing the boom.
II. The US Economy Continues To Perform Well
The economy and, therefore, earnings remain fundamentally resilient,
which continues to support the bull market. With the midterms behind us
and political uncertainty receding, investors can refocus on these strong

fundamentals. Here are the highlights of today's upbeat economic
indicators:
(1) ISM Services PMI. The ISM-NM PMI edged down modestly to 54.5 in
October, marking its 28th consecutive month of expansion (chart).
Underlying demand remained solid, with new orders and business activity
both comfortably above the 50.0 expansion threshold. Encouragingly, the
employment index rose from 50.1 to 51.4, marking its second consecutive
month of expansion.

### Page 5

Inflation is still a concern. The ISM prices-paid indexes remained elevated
in October (chart).
(2) Labor market. The average of the ISM employment indexes rose to
51.9 in October, its highest reading in roughly three years (chart). ADP
reported an 86,000 increase in private payrolls in October, beating
consensus expectations of 67,000.

### Page 6

(3) Trade balance. The latest trade data point to resilient US and global
economic growth. Merchandise imports rose 2.4% m/m in September to a
record $3.44 trillion annualized, while exports increased 2.5% m/m to
$2.09 trillion. The merchandise trade deficit widened to $1.35 trillion
annualized (chart).
Nominal and real US merchandise exports plus imports rose 21.1% y/y
and 10.1%, respectively, in September (chart). That suggests global trade
remains firmly in expansion territory and is consistent with strong global

economic growth.

### Page 7

Selected technology imports reached a record $61.3 billion in September,
reflecting the AI buildout’s heavy reliance on imported semiconductors,
servers, and other hardware (chart).

may be featured in the next "Just Ask Dr. Ed!"

clause.

Historical day last Friday in Taiwan with index down close to 10% before making a 2k

points climb intraday.  Index futures also touched limit down briefly - last time it
happened was during Jul'26 degrossing. Due to such volatility, FSC has decided to
intervene the market. The Desk stayed back on Friday to put out below notes. Special
thanks to our trading desk head Elaine Kuo, sales Jill Huang, and compliance Daniel
Lin.
Taiwan’s NATIONAL STABILIZATION FUND held a meeting in the afternoon
[2026/11/6] and agreed to activate the National Stabilization Fund (NSF) with

NT$500b or US$15.2b usable capital to stabilize the mkt. NSF had a good track
record in its previous 9 interventions. The mkt rallied 6% on avg in the following week
after announcement, and +22% over one year. However, 6 out of 9 times experienced
additional 5%~28% drawdowns within 6 months after activation.
While National Stabilization Fund announcement provides a sentiment boost to the
stock market, the actual deployed capital could be limited - only NT$0.76b~122.7b

(US$23m~3.7b) were actually deployed in the past 9 instances vs. NSF NT$500b
(US$15.2b) usable capital. That being said, from previous experience, NSF tends to
buy large cap tech/ financials with heavy index weighting (see below).

Furthermore, Taiwan's Financial Supervisory Commission (FSC) announced five
market stabilization measures effective November 9, 2026, without specifying an
end date, to calm stock market volatility following sharp selloffs in the Taiwan Market:
1. Intraday Short-Selling Cap: Lowered the daily short sell limit from 30% to 3% of
30-day average daily trading volume (exemptions apply to hedging and market-
making activities).
2. Margin Requirement Hike: Increased the minimum margin requirement for

retails’ margin short selling listed stocks (融券保證⾦成數) from 90% to 130%.
11/9/26, 8:28 AM GS SALESTRADING: Taiwan National Stabilization Fund and Tighter Short Sell Restriction | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/18f8509b-7aa3-4cdb-8093-13ae5c123cb2 2/9

### Page 3

3. Expanded Collateral Scope: Allowed investors to use alternative liquid collateral
(such as foreign securities held via sub-brokerage accounts) to meet margin calls
and cover financing shortfalls.
4. Encourage Share Buybacks: Urged listed companies to initiate treasury share
buybacks based on their financial and capital conditions.
5. Encourage Investor Communications: Encouraged listed companies to host
institutional investor conferences and earnings presentations to reinforce

corporate fundamentals.
These measures are very similar to last time in 2025 April. Some investors concern
that lowered daily short sell limit (for institutional investors’ short selling) and higher
margin requirement (for retail investors’ margin short selling) will hurt market
participation and liquidity.
